# VoidFall Unity Parity Matrix

- Browser-compatible save-schema gate (2026-08-16): Unity's browser exporter writes the four shared React v5 `RunRecord` fields (`damageDealt`, `damageTaken`, `weapons`, and `weaponDamage`) plus the richer `supports`, `late`, and `evolved` snapshots. React v5's sanitizer ignores those extra keys, while Unity's importer preserves them, so export -> import remains lossless for the native record shape. Focused EditMode passed 1/1 in `D:/VoidFall-builds/TestResults/browser-save-schema-focused-2922.xml`; full EditMode passed 63/63 in `D:/VoidFall-builds/TestResults/browser-save-schema-editmode-2923.xml`; full PlayMode passed 377/377 in `D:/VoidFall-builds/TestResults/browser-save-schema-playmode-2924.xml` in 289.10 seconds; and the rebuilt player succeeded in `D:/VoidFall-builds/TestResults/windows-build-2925.log` as `D:/VoidFall-builds/VoidFall-windows-validation-2925.exe` (667,648 bytes). Its corrected D3D11/GTX 1060 `productionMax` smoke exited 0 and wrote `D:/VoidFall-builds/TestResults/windows-player-stress-save-schema-2925-correct.json` with 191 enemies, 2 bosses, and a 6.588 ms final frame EMA; this short smoke is a boot/regression check, not an RTX 3080 performance claim. Workspace and validation-clone exporter/test files are SHA-256 identical. Android/device and exact browser raster/font equivalence remain open.

- Settings quality-label focus gate (2026-08-16): Unity now gives the browser-equivalent settings quality row label area focus behavior, so clicking text outside the custom select focuses `VoidFall.SettingsQualitySelect` while clicks inside the select remain handled by the select control. The focused predicate regression passed 1/1 in `D:/VoidFall-builds/TestResults/settings-quality-label-focused-2918.xml`; full EditMode passed 62/62 in `D:/VoidFall-builds/TestResults/settings-quality-label-editmode-2919.xml`; full PlayMode passed 377/377 in `D:/VoidFall-builds/TestResults/settings-quality-label-playmode-2920.xml` in 289.07 seconds; and the rebuilt player succeeded in `D:/VoidFall-builds/TestResults/windows-build-2921.log` as `D:/VoidFall-builds/VoidFall-windows-validation-2921.exe` (667,648 bytes). Its corrected D3D11/GTX 1060 `productionMax` smoke exited 0 and wrote `D:/VoidFall-builds/TestResults/windows-player-stress-settings-2921-correct.json` with 191 enemies, 2 bosses, and a 6.750 ms final frame EMA; this short smoke is a boot/regression check, not an RTX 3080 performance claim. Workspace and validation-clone runtime/test files are SHA-256 identical. Android/device and exact browser raster/font equivalence remain open.

- Settings toggle row hit-area gate (2026-08-16): Unity now treats the entire settings toggle row as the clickable control, matching React's `ToggleSetting` button wrapper instead of responding only to the small switch. The focused row-predicate regression passed 1/1 in `D:/VoidFall-builds/TestResults/settings-toggle-focused-2914-rerun.xml`; full EditMode passed 62/62 in `D:/VoidFall-builds/TestResults/settings-toggle-editmode-2915.xml`; full PlayMode passed 376/376 in `D:/VoidFall-builds/TestResults/settings-toggle-playmode-2916.xml` in 296.66 seconds; and the rebuilt player succeeded in `D:/VoidFall-builds/TestResults/windows-build-2917.log` as `D:/VoidFall-builds/VoidFall-windows-validation-2917.exe` (667,648 bytes). Its corrected D3D11/GTX 1060 `productionMax` smoke exited 0 and wrote `D:/VoidFall-builds/TestResults/windows-player-stress-settings-2917-correct.json` with 191 enemies, 2 bosses, and a 6.935 ms final frame EMA; this short smoke is a boot/regression check, not an RTX 3080 performance claim. Workspace and validation-clone runtime/test files are SHA-256 identical. Android/device and exact browser raster/font equivalence remain open.

- Settings save-first transaction gate (2026-08-16): Unity now stages a copy of the prior settings, saves the changed profile before applying the live audio/quality state, and restores the previous value plus live controller state if persistence fails, matching React's `updateSettings` save-first contract. `CommitSettings` now reports storage failures through the existing menu notice instead of leaking an exception. The focused failure-path regression passed 1/1 in `D:/VoidFall-builds/TestResults/settings-save-focused-2910.xml`; full EditMode passed 62/62 in `D:/VoidFall-builds/TestResults/settings-save-editmode-2911.xml`; full PlayMode passed 375/375 in `D:/VoidFall-builds/TestResults/settings-save-playmode-2912.xml` in 293.13 seconds; and the rebuilt player succeeded in `D:/VoidFall-builds/TestResults/windows-build-2913.log` as `D:/VoidFall-builds/VoidFall-windows-validation-2913.exe` (667,648 bytes). Its corrected D3D11/GTX 1060 `productionMax` smoke exited cleanly and wrote `D:/VoidFall-builds/TestResults/windows-player-stress-settings-2913-correct.json` with 191 enemies, 2 bosses, and a 6.712 ms final frame EMA; the short smoke is a boot/regression check, not an RTX 3080 performance claim. Android/device and exact browser raster/font equivalence remain open.

- Run-start toast visibility gate (2026-08-15): Unity's initial playing-phase toast is covered against the browser-rendered contract: `StartRun` queues the source `Run started` message, the HUD view is enabled during the initial playing phase, and the displayed text is uppercased to `RUN STARTED` like the browser's `.toast { text-transform: uppercase; }`. The focused PlayMode regression passed 1/1 in `D:/VoidFall-builds/TestResults/toast-focused-2907.xml`; the full validation-clone PlayMode suite passed 374/374 in `D:/VoidFall-builds/TestResults/toast-playmode-2908.xml` in 294.27 seconds. This was a test/evidence correction, not a runtime behavior change; the current 2904 Windows player remains the latest packaged runtime. A spawned unfocused player can auto-pause before a screenshot, so visible capture comparisons must keep the player focused; Android/device and exact raster/font equivalence remain open.

- Touch/UI input boundary gate (2026-08-15): Unity now checks the primary touch's UI pointer capture before starting the floating joystick and retains that block until release, matching React's canvas-only `pointerdown` listener so tapping a pause/menu control cannot also start movement. The focused EditMode regression passed 1/1 in `D:/VoidFall-builds/TestResults/touch-ui-focused-2901.xml`; full EditMode passed 62/62 in `D:/VoidFall-builds/TestResults/touch-ui-editmode-2902.xml`; full PlayMode passed 373/373 in `D:/VoidFall-builds/TestResults/touch-ui-playmode-2903.xml`; and the updated Windows player packaged successfully in `D:/VoidFall-builds/TestResults/windows-build-2904.log` as `D:/VoidFall-builds/VoidFall-windows-validation-2904.exe` (667,648 bytes). The workspace/validation-clone runtime and test files are SHA-256 identical. Physical touch, safe-area, lifecycle, and Android-device validation remain open because the Android module and device are unavailable.

- HUD pause-control parity gate (2026-08-15): Unity now keeps the pause control active whenever the source-equivalent HUD is visible, including immediately after a run starts at `_time == 0`, matching React's playing-phase gate. The focused PlayMode regression passed 1/1 in `D:/VoidFall-builds/TestResults/hud-pause-focused-2895.xml`; full PlayMode passed 373/373 in `D:/VoidFall-builds/TestResults/hud-pause-playmode-2896.xml`; and the rebuilt Windows player succeeded in `D:/VoidFall-builds/TestResults/windows-build-2897.log` as `D:/VoidFall-builds/VoidFall-windows-validation-2897.exe` (667,648 bytes). Its canonical D3D11 productionMax player smoke exited 0, loaded fixture `4d5e955`, held 192 enemies / 2 bosses, and wrote `D:/VoidFall-builds/TestResults/windows-player-stress-threaded-2897-nobatch.json` with a 22.118 ms final frame EMA; a repeat measured 22.060 ms, while the prior build under the same launch mode measured 22.045 ms, so the HUD change shows no runtime regression in this comparison. The workspace/validation-clone runtime and test files are SHA-256 identical. Browser verification remains green from the latest `npm run verify`; exact raster/device and Android gates remain open.

- Stress-output argument parity gate (2026-08-15): `StressBenchmarkProbe` now accepts both `-name=value` and separate-token values through the same parser, and the canonical `-vfoutput=...` player invocation writes the requested D: artifact instead of silently falling back to `%LOCALAPPDATA%`. The focused EditMode regression passed 1/1 in `D:/VoidFall-builds/TestResults/stress-arg-focused-2892.xml`; full EditMode passed 61/61 in `D:/VoidFall-builds/TestResults/stress-arg-editmode-2893.xml`; the rebuilt Windows player succeeded in `D:/VoidFall-builds/TestResults/windows-build-2894.log` as `D:/VoidFall-builds/VoidFall-windows-validation-2894.exe`; and the no-batchmode threaded D3D11 productionMax smoke exited 0, loaded fixture `4d5e955`, held 192 enemies / 2 bosses, and wrote `D:/VoidFall-builds/TestResults/windows-player-stress-threaded-2894-nobatch.json` from `D:/VoidFall-builds/TestResults/windows-player-stress-threaded-2894-nobatch.log` with a 2.998 ms frame EMA. The workspace/validation-clone parser and test files are SHA-256 identical. Unity's batchmode path is intentionally excluded from the performance comparison because it measured 19.85 ms on the same build; it is a headless build/test mode, not the canonical player runtime. Browser verification remains green; exact raster/device and Android gates remain open.

- Windows build and player stress gate (2026-08-15): the validation clone packaged successfully with Unity's native `Unity.com -buildWindows64Player` route while `TEMP`/`TMP` pointed at D:, avoiding the earlier C: `database or disk is full` failure without deleting storage. Build log: `D:/VoidFall-builds/TestResults/windows-build-2890.log`; executable: `D:/VoidFall-builds/VoidFall-windows-validation-2890.exe` (667,648 bytes). The built player exited 0 after the source-defined `productionMax` benchmark, loaded fixture `4d5e955`, reached 192 enemies and 2 bosses, and produced the copied result `D:/VoidFall-builds/TestResults/windows-player-stress-threaded-2890.json`; the canonical threaded D3D11 sample held 192 enemies / 2 bosses at 2.713 ms frame EMA with no targeted exception, assertion, or fatal markers. A separate non-threaded D3D11 launch measured 18.755 ms, confirming the earlier high sample was a launch-mode artifact rather than a code regression. Both runs used the current host's GTX 1060 6 GB GPU, so they are not performance claims for the incoming RTX 3080 machine. The build log contains a non-fatal Unity licensing access-token refresh message but resolves entitlements and ends `Build Finished, Result: Success.`

- Windows host soak follow-up (2026-08-14): current build `unity/Builds/Windows/VoidFall-menu-panel-2059.exe` was profiled at 1600x900/D3D12 for 120 seconds against fixture `4d5e955` in `unity/TestResults/VoidFall-2061-menu-panel-120s-profile.log`; all 24 five-second samples stayed alive, the app log recorded no exception/fatal/crash/assertion markers, and combined working set ranged 384.9-561.2 MB while CPU time ranged 5.59-7.34 seconds. A process-isolated 30-second follow-up in `unity/TestResults/VoidFall-2062-process-profile.log` showed the game process step from 381.8 to 554.7 MB around 20 seconds then remain flat through the remaining samples; the crash handler stayed at 6.7 MB. This is a current GTX 1060/Windows host signal, not Android memory or frame-budget compliance; target-device profiling and the cause of the startup working-set step remain open.
- Renderer comparison follow-up (2026-08-14): the same build, fixture, resolution, and 30-second process-isolated sampling under a command line verified with `-force-d3d11` recorded 194.5 MB at 5 seconds, a single step to 245.8 MB at 10 seconds, then a flat plateau through 30 seconds in `unity/TestResults/VoidFall-2063-d3d11-process-profile.log`. D3D11 reproduces the one-time step at a lower absolute working set than the earlier D3D12 run, so this is not evidence of a continuing managed-memory leak; the responsible Unity/render initialization allocation, target-device memory, and frame-budget compliance remain open.

- Source asset audit (2026-08-14): recursive source scan found no external image, model, or audio binaries under `src` or `public`. Browser gameplay visuals are generated by `src/game/sprites.ts` and `src/game/arena_visuals.ts`, while sound is procedural in `src/game/audio.ts`; Unity therefore uses the corresponding procedural ports plus the imported UI SVG/raster assets under `unity/VoidFall/Assets/VoidFall/Resources/VoidFall`. No missing source binary import was found. Exact Canvas2D raster/compositing and WebAudio output equivalence remain open.

Source of truth: current React/TypeScript implementation. `Reference` means inspected but not yet ported. `Ported` requires Unity code plus a matching test or runtime evidence. `Blocked` means external evidence or software is missing.

Latest verified gate (2026-08-11): PlayMode `unity/VoidFall/TestResults/playmode-526.xml` passed 77/77; EditMode `unity/VoidFall/TestResults/editmode-525.xml` passed 38/38; Windows build `unity/VoidFall/TestResults/windows-build-527.log` succeeded; and `unity/VoidFall/TestResults/VoidFall-fixture-sync-528-run.log` stayed alive for 45 seconds with 0 exception/crash/assertion/error markers. The browser gate `npm run verify` also passes formatting, typecheck, 23 test files/222 tests, and production build. The 120-second profile `unity/VoidFall/TestResults/VoidFall-fixture-sync-530-profile120.log` stayed alive with 0 issue markers and settled at about 414.7 MiB working set. The latest slices include synchronized source fixtures in both Unity locations, application lifecycle pause/audio behavior, the 2.6-second evolution reveal, browser-equivalent arrow-key movement with normalized diagonals, the browser's 2-second adaptive-quality warm-up, resize/DPI settling semantics, clean overview Unicode labels, and source-matched level-up card copy/rank pips; exact browser Canvas2D/audio/device behavior and Android verification remain open.

Current verified gate (2026-08-11; supersedes older historical summaries below): PlayMode `playmode-458.xml` 73/73, EditMode `editmode-457.xml` 38/38, Windows build `windows-build-459.log` successful, and smoke `VoidFall-settings-459-run.log` fixture-loaded/responsive for 45 seconds with no exception/crash/assertion/error markers. Menus now use source-inspired rounded 9-slice gradients for panels/buttons/toggles/sliders/scrollbars, hover/active states, spacing, and text contrast. Settings now match source toggle labels/details, persist immediately, and quantize touch size to 0.05 within 0.75–1.35; exact browser font assets, Canvas2D/input rendering, and device screenshots remain open. Telemetry serialization now rounds browser event/sample times and display floats, floors held XP and minimum FPS, and normalizes arena summaries; the newest 2,160 samples are retained by evicting the oldest on overflow. Telemetry FPS now uses browser half-up rounding. Damage-number display now uses browser half-up rounding at exact `.5` values. Normal enemy kills now award the browser's flat 10 score independently of enemy XP. Browser floor boundaries cover buffered/ground XP, run time, telemetry filenames, saved/displayed time, and telemetry XP values; Harvester limits and repair amounts use browser half-up rounding. Elite rewards and roster XP use browser half-up rounding. Per-weapon and total damage values use browser-compatible rounding at save/telemetry/display boundaries. Browser oscillator pitch hints no longer stretch Unity cue duration; source-backed Gem/Fuse variants remain duration-stable. Bullet and hostile-shot order now mirror browser compact-array swap-with-last removal and append across pooled slots. Enemy and meteor order mirror browser compact-array swap-with-last removal and append across pooled slots; boss charge/slam/projectile/summon counts and beam sweep speed use source encounter-cycle scaling; Orbit/Hollow Blade use exact source neighborhoods; area blasts, Bomb, Exploder, revive, rail-wake, and Overload effects preserve browser snapshot/scope semantics across pooled-slot reuse, with intro-boss rejection, source revive radius, rail cadence, the revive-versus-terminal defeat cue branch, Workshop mobility trail phase, browser camera-trauma amplitude, and source-radius Power pulse.

Latest verified slice (2026-08-11): PlayMode `unity/VoidFall/TestResults/playmode-330.xml` passed 57/57; EditMode `unity/VoidFall/TestResults/editmode-329.xml` passed 33/33; Windows build `unity/VoidFall/TestResults/windows-build-331.log` succeeded; and `unity/VoidFall/TestResults/VoidFall-collision-order-332-run.log` loaded the generated fixture, stayed responsive through the smoke cutoff, and contained no exception/crash/assertion/error markers. CollisionGrid now preserves browser FIFO insertion order within cells and browser X-then-Y cell traversal, preventing pooled-slot order from changing first-hit resolution. The Void arena now has one pooled `MeshTopology.Lines` grid with browser 96-unit spacing, source tint, camera-relative coverage, and Void-only visibility. Audio now prebuilds browser-timed Gem variants for pickup steps 0–24 and Fuse Warning variants for stages 0–5, preserving source cue durations instead of using playback pitch to change frequency. Workshop preview still includes source-shaped cyan gradient trails, browser dash lengths, cached marker/stroke glow passes, and source-sized operative/ring sprites with Power drawn last while retaining the 600x340 backdrop, centered layers, source drift, and reduced-motion support. Runtime HUD includes browser-shaped XP/integrity/clock/metrics, live `LOADOUT`/`SUP`/`LATE` summaries, Overclock countdown/bar feedback, clickable pause control routed through Unity's UI event system, and source-positioned support/late-upgrade strips. Source `lighter` compositing has a dedicated premultiplied additive sprite material for player bullets, Hollow Blade, meteor hit/core overlays, and meteor-owned hostile shots while normal hostile shots remain source-over, alongside the existing particle path.

- Latest boss-contact-phase gate (2026-08-13): Unity now applies a boss's charge damage on player contact only during the browser's active charge step; charge wind-up and recovery retain normal contact damage while the selected attack remains queued. Focused D3D11 PlayMode `unity/TestResults/playmode-1481-boss-charge-contact.xml` passed 1/1; full EditMode `unity/TestResults/editmode-1482.xml` passed 41/41; full headless PlayMode `unity/TestResults/playmode-1483.xml` passed 199/199; Windows packaging reported `Build Finished, Result: Success` in `unity/TestResults/windows-build-1484.log`; and the 45-second player smoke `unity/TestResults/VoidFall-boss-contact-1485-player.log` stayed alive, loaded fixture `4d5e955`, recorded 0 severe markers, and left the exact player process exited. Browser authority `npm run verify` also passed formatting, TypeScript, 23 test files / 222 tests, and the production Vite build. Exact WebAudio graph/mixing/spatial behavior, Canvas2D raster/compositing, and Android/device profiling remain open.
